class="html-attribute-value">bb_h1</span>"&gt;</span>I Knew It! - PREVIEW<span class="html-tag">&lt;/div&gt;</span>Hi, MKev here.<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span>Over the years I've been inspired by countless mods. Some of them stopped working after the recent updates, others haven't seen an update since B41. That's what got me to build my own little quality of life mod. I coded and checked it with the help of AI. I hope it fills a few gaps and makes your games a bit more enjoyable.<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;div <span class="html-attribute-name">class</span>="<span class="html-attribute-value">bb_h2</span>"&gt;</span>New skill book and media system<span class="html-tag">&lt;/div&gt;</span>Books, magazines, VHS tapes, CDs and other readable or recorded media get small icons wherever the game knows whether you have read or learned them. So you can see at a glance what an item does for your character:<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;img <span class="html-attribute-name">src</span>="<a class="html-attribute-value html-resource-link" target="_blank" href="https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/15250581217865739798/4263D091C2582101EA664E8BE83CB36C965944EB/" rel="noreferrer noopener">https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/15250581217865739798/4263D091C2582101EA664E8BE83CB36C965944EB/</a>" /&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span>Skill books and recipe magazines from other mods are recognized automatically when they use the normal game data. Documents from Working Knowledge are recognized too, when that mod is active. Also adds short title prefixes like <span class="html-tag">&lt;b&gt;</span>[Skillbook]<span class="html-tag">&lt;/b&gt;</span>, <span class="html-tag">&lt;b&gt;</span>[Skill]<span class="html-tag">&lt;/b&gt;</span> or <span class="html-tag">&lt;b&gt;</span>[Recipes]<span class="html-tag">&lt;/b&gt;</span> and an optional icon legend in the tooltip.<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;div <span class="html-attribute-name">class</span>="<span class="html-attribute-value">bb_h2</span>"&gt;</span>Special feature: Easy Picking<span class="html-tag">&lt;/div&gt;</span>Ever wanted to grab that key from the purse inside a closet without emptying the whole closet? No worries, I've got you covered.<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span>Open a bag, purse, key ring or first aid kit that sits inside a closet, a corpse or a car, and take just what you need: with the small [&amp;gt;] arrow in the loot list, or by right-clicking it and choosing &amp;quot;Take contents&amp;quot;.<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;img <span class="html-attribute-name">src</span>="<a class="html-attribute-value html-resource-link" target="_blank" href="https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/9991556401096789665/538961632B8B67C628B1DD5E346F3D108C3C6EE3/" rel="noreferrer noopener">https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/9991556401096789665/538961632B8B67C628B1DD5E346F3D108C3C6EE3/</a>" /&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;b&gt;</span>Fair warning:<span class="html-tag">&lt;/b&gt;</span> Easy Picking has not been tested extensively yet, especially in multiplayer, and you may run into an occasional error. That's why it can be switched off in the Sandbox options (&amp;quot;Easy Picking&amp;quot;, on by default). If something feels off, just turn it off and please let me know.<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;div <span class="html-attribute-name">class</span>="<span class="html-attribute-value">bb_h2</span>"&gt;</span>Options<span class="html-tag">&lt;/div&gt;</span>The main features can be turned on or off in the Sandbox options (page &amp;quot;I Knew It!&amp;quot;).<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;div <span class="html-attribute-name">class</span>="<span class="html-attribute-value">bb_h2</span>"&gt;</span>Not compatible / please don't use together<span class="html-tag">&lt;/div&gt;</span>These mods change the same things, so please don't run them together with I Knew It!:<span class="html-tag">&lt;br&gt;</span><span class="html-tag">&lt;ul <span class="html-attribute-name">class</span>="<span
